# API ML Feature Ledger: catalog vs. repository

This ledger checks each entry of `apiml-features` (the docs-derived catalog, docs-site commit `d442d4358`) against this repository.

- **Repository state:** `zowe/api-layer`, branch `v3.x.x`, commit `99a855c3f` (version `3.6.3-SNAPSHOT`), checked 2026-11-01.
- **What was checked:**
  - Does the feature exist in this repo?
  - Are its keys, endpoints, headers, message IDs, job suffixes, ports and defaults named as the docs say?
  - Is each config key actually wired: zowe.yaml → `ZWE_configs_*` → `start.sh` → Spring property → Java code?
  - Is it wired in both multi-service mode (`<component>-package/.../bin/start.sh`) and single-service mode (`apiml-package/.../bin/start.sh`)?
- **What was not checked:** whether the implementation is correct.
- **Sources consulted:**
  - `*-package/src/main/resources/bin/start.sh`
  - `scripts/*.sh`
  - `*-package/src/main/resources/schemas/*-config.json`
  - `manifest.yaml`
  - `application.yml`
  - Java `@Value` / `@ConfigurationProperties` usages
  - `*-log-messages.yml`
  - enabler sources
  - git history
- **Out of reach:** anything implemented outside this repo is marked `EXTERNAL`. That covers zwe/launcher/`defaults.yaml` (zowe-install-packaging), ESM setup, ZSS/ZIS, z/OSMF workflows, AT-TLS policy, Kubernetes manifests, Zowe CLI and Zowe Explorer. Where this repo has a hook into such a feature, the hook was verified.
- **Confidence:**
  - Each catalog chunk was checked by a separate agent pass.
  - The headline findings in section 0 were re-verified by hand against the files cited.
  - Other rows cite evidence paths; spot-check them before acting.

**Feature verdicts:**

| Verdict | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `OK` | Matches. |
| `MISMATCH` | Implemented, but at least one key, name, endpoint, default or message ID differs. |
| `PARTIAL` | Some of the functionality or keys are missing or unwired. |
| `NOT FOUND` | Should be in this repo but is absent. |
| `EXTERNAL` | Lives in another repo. |
| `CONCEPT` | Overview entry with nothing concrete to verify. |
| `UNCLEAR` | Could not be determined. |

**Key statuses:**

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `OK` | Key exists and is wired. |
| `WRONG NAME` | The real key has a different name. |
| `NOT WIRED` | Code reads the key but nothing maps it from zowe.yaml, or a mapping exists but nothing reads the key. |
| `MODULITH GAP` | Works in only one of the two deployment modes. |
| `DEFAULT DIFFERS` | The documented default differs from the code. |
| `NOT FOUND` | Key appears nowhere. |
| `EXTERNAL` | Read outside this repo. |
| `UNCLEAR` | Could not be determined. |

## 0. Headline findings (re-verified by hand)

1. **Typos in single-service `start.sh` env var names**
   - `ZWE_components_apicatalog_*` should be `api_catalog` (`apiml-package/src/main/resources/bin/start.sh:307-314`).
   - Three variables lack the `apiml_` segment: `ZWE_components_gateway_gateway_registry_*` and `ZWE_configs_gateway_registry_*` (`:325,329,331`).
   - Effect: in single-service mode these zowe.yaml keys are ignored: `components.api-catalog.apiml.catalog.customStyle.*`, `components.api-catalog.apiml.catalog.hide.serviceInfo`, and `components.gateway.apiml.gateway.registry.{maxSimultaneousRequests,refreshIntervalMs,metadataKeyAllowList}`.
2. **Other single-service gaps**
   - `apiml.security.forwardHeader.trustedProxies` is not mapped in `apiml-package` start.sh at all.
   - The `apiml-config.json` schema declares `apiml.internal.discovery.*` and `apiml.storage.*`, but start.sh reads `internal.discovery.*` and `storage.*` (for example `apiml-package/.../start.sh:335-336`).
   - Some per-component keys are ignored, for example `components.zaas.*`, and `debug` / `health.protected` / `sslDebug` for catalog, caching and discovery.
3. **`auth.provider` default conflict**
   - Every schema has `enum: [zosmf, saf]` with default `saf` (`zaas-package/src/main/resources/schemas/zaas-config.json:84-89`).
   - `start.sh`, `application.yml` and the Java code fall back to `zosmf`.
   - The schema enum also rejects `dummy`, which the code accepts.
4. **Keys passed but read by nothing**
   - Gateway `server.webSocket.{maxIdleTimeout,connectTimeout,asyncWriteTimeout}`.
   - ZAAS `apiml.security.jwtInitializerTimeout`: no Java reader since #5015, the wait is hard-coded to 1 min, yet message ZWEAG108E still says "5 minutes".
   - `server.webSocket.requestBufferSize` actually sets the WebSocket max frame payload length. Its default is `24576` in gateway start.sh but `8192` in apiml start.sh and the schema (`gateway-package/.../start.sh:250`, `apiml-package/.../start.sh:459`).
5. **Documented keys that do not exist in the code**
   - `components.gateway.apiml.service.allowEncodedSlashes`: replaced by `apiml.security.enableStrictUrlValidation`, default `true`, so encoded slashes are rejected by default.
   - All `ribbon.*` retry keys.
   - `apiml.gateway.timeoutMillis`.
   - `apiml.gateway.externalProtocol` (removed).
   - `components.discovery.alternativeStaticApiDefinitionsDirectories`.
   - `apiml.loadBalancer.distribute`.
6. **Per-service metadata the v3 Gateway does not read**
   - `apiml.headersToIgnore`, `apiml.response.compress`, `apiml.response.compressRoutes`, `apiml.response.headers`.
   - `apiml.readTimeout`: the real key is `apiml.responseTimeout`.
   - `apiml.connectionManagerTimeout`, `apiml.okToRetryOnAllOperations`.
7. **Caching Service storage**
   - The multi-service default is `inMemory` (`caching-service-package/.../start.sh:156`). `infinispan` is the default only in single-service mode, where `validate.sh` rejects every other mode.
   - `storage.redis.*` and the VSAM sub-keys cannot be set from zowe.yaml; they work only through env vars.
   - The key-exchange port is 7601, not 7118.
8. **`debug-control` profile cannot be set:** the `spring.profiles.active` schema pattern `^[a-zA-Z0-9]+(,[a-zA-Z0-9]+)*$` rejects the hyphen (`gateway-package/src/main/resources/schemas/gateway-config.json:742`).
9. **Wrong names in the docs**
   - Single-service job suffix is `AG`, not `GW`.
   - The Gateway is Spring Cloud Gateway, not Zuul.
   - Auth endpoints live under `/gateway/api/v1/auth/*`, except the SAF check, which is `/gateway/auth/check` (`gateway-service/.../AuthEndpointConfig.java:145-162`).
   - The z/OSMF service ID key is `auth.zosmf.serviceId`.
   - The Eureka threshold property is `eureka.server.renewalPercentThreshold`.
   - Message ZWEAM511 is cited as AML0105, and `SEC0002` is not in this repo.
   - The PAT revoke path is `/revoke/tokens/user`.
10. **Version drift:** this branch runs Spring Boot 4.1.1 / Spring 7, but the docs say Boot 3. `zaas-client` targets Java 17, but the docs say Java 8.
11. **Same key, different defaults in schema and start.sh:** the api-catalog port is `7554` in the schema but `7552` in start.sh and the manifest.

Per-entry detail is in section 2; per-key detail is in section 3.
